- **sai-1-spec**: Turns an initial description of a need or feature into a clear proposal. The proposal explains what should be achieved and defines criteria that can later be used to check whether the result meets expectations. ([mmadariaga/shared-ai](https://github.com/mmadariaga/shared-ai/blob/HEAD/docs/commands/sai-1-spec.md) · ★9)
- **sai-1-spec**: Write or refine a change's proposal.md and specs/ from a /sai-explore Ready to Propose block, or from a change name plus feedback. Stops before design; running /sai-2-design approves the specs. ([lhorcajada/RFFM](https://github.com/lhorcajada/RFFM/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sai-1-spec.md))
- **sai-2-design**: Turns an approved proposal into an understandable, actionable technical design. It describes the chosen solution, its important decisions, its trade-offs, and an ordered task list. ([mmadariaga/shared-ai](https://github.com/mmadariaga/shared-ai/blob/HEAD/docs/commands/sai-2-design.md) · ★9)
- **sai-2-design**: Design a change into design.md, tasks.md, and interfaces.md — running it approves the specs; --overview-lang also writes change-overview.md. Stops before /sai-3-implement. ([lhorcajada/RFFM](https://github.com/lhorcajada/RFFM/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sai-2-design.md))
- **sai-3-implement**: Implements a change's planning artifacts (`proposal.md`, `specs/`, `design.md`, `tasks.md`, `interfaces.md`) from start to finish with no human in the loop. It writes the tests, writes the code, runs the checks, and makes one local commit per step. It works on Claude Code and opencode. ([mmadariaga/shared-ai](https://github.com/mmadariaga/shared-ai/blob/HEAD/docs/commands/sai-3-implement.md) · ★9)
- **sai-3-implement**: Implement a change's plan end to end with no human in the loop — live RED and GREEN workers, per-step tests, and one local commit per step. ([lhorcajada/RFFM](https://github.com/lhorcajada/RFFM/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sai-3-implement.md))
- **sai-4-review**: Reviews a change's diff against its parent branch in a fresh worker and writes `openspec/changes/{change}/review.md`. It only reviews: it fixes no finding. `/sai-3-implement` applies open findings, so fixes keep one owner. It works on Claude Code and opencode. ([mmadariaga/shared-ai](https://github.com/mmadariaga/shared-ai/blob/HEAD/docs/commands/sai-4-review.md) · ★9)
- **sai-4-review**: Review a change's diff in a fresh worker and write review.md, with no fixes and no human in the loop; /sai-3-implement applies the findings you keep. Accepts --parent-branch &lt;branch&gt;. ([lhorcajada/RFFM](https://github.com/lhorcajada/RFFM/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sai-4-review.md))
- **sai-5-security**: Looks for vulnerabilities and security risks in the completed changes. It explains the impact of each finding, identifies the exact location where it appears, and links it to known references when useful. ([mmadariaga/shared-ai](https://github.com/mmadariaga/shared-ai/blob/HEAD/docs/commands/sai-5-security.md) · ★9)
- **sai-5-security**: Audit a change for security flaws — SAST on the diff vs parent (or --full / --path), SCA when dependency manifests change — into openspec/changes/{change-name}/security.md. Accepts --full, --path &lt;dir&gt;, --parent-branch &lt;branch&gt;. ([lhorcajada/RFFM](https://github.com/lhorcajada/RFFM/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sai-5-security.md))
- **sai-6-performance**: Detects performance problems introduced by the changes. It looks for operations that could slow the system down, consume too many resources, or grow without control. ([mmadariaga/shared-ai](https://github.com/mmadariaga/shared-ai/blob/HEAD/docs/commands/sai-6-performance.md) · ★9)
- **sai-6-performance**: Audit a change for performance problems — backend, frontend, database, and queue tiers on the diff vs parent (or --full / --path), optional --runtime diagnostics — into openspec/changes/{change-name}/performance.md. Accepts --full, --path &lt;dir&gt;, --tier &lt;tier&gt;, --runtime, --parent-branch &lt;branch&gt;. ([lhorcajada/RFFM](https://github.com/lhorcajada/RFFM/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sai-6-performance.md))
- **sai-6-security**: Structured Security Audit Prompt — SAST + SCA on the diff vs parent branch (or full repo / path), produces openspec/changes/{change-name}/security.md ([mmadariaga/shared-ai](https://github.com/mmadariaga/shared-ai/blob/HEAD/test/fixtures/retired-audit-definitions/claude/commands/sai-6-security.md) · ★9 · 2 copies)

- **sai-7-accessibility**: Audit a change's UI against WCAG 2.2 AA — static review of the diff vs parent (or --full / --path), optional --runtime axe/pa11y/Lighthouse — into openspec/changes/{change-name}/accessibility.md. - **sai-archive**: Checks that a change is ready and moves it from the active changes area to the archive of completed changes, keeping the active workspace clean. ([mmadariaga/shared-ai](https://github.com/mmadariaga/shared-ai/blob/HEAD/docs/commands/sai-archive.md) · ★9)

- **salary**: **The Anchoring Effect**: The first number mentioned in a compensation conversation becomes the anchor. If a candidate says "$120K" in a recruiter screen, the final offer will orbit $120K — regardless of market rate. This makes the recruiter screen the highest-leverage comp moment. By the time `negotiate` fires (post-offer), the anchor is set. salary coaches the moment that sets the anchor. ([noamseg/interview-coach-skill](https://github.com/noamseg/interview-coach-skill/blob/HEAD/references/commands/salary.md) · ★2.5k · 9 copies)
